Independent review and owner adoption remain separate.","rationale":"The official later instrument expressly revokes this exact numbered executive order as a whole, rather than only inconsistent provisions. Complete original scope and the acting section must be independently tested; historical implementation and accrued consequences are separated from continuing original commands. Current status is separate from the exact first-cessation date. The contemporaneous order text establishes a complete revocation before the scientific cutoff; no exact effective instant is assumed from publication or signing alone. Where EO14148 rescinds the later revoking instrument, its full text must be checked for actual affirmative re-enactment, not presumed revival. For the COVID group, the inspected EO14148 does not list EO14122; the earlier coordinator premise saying otherwise was rejected. The complete target and actor preserve no independent post-revocation target regime on their face, while specific implementation, accrued consequences and earliest-ending research remain explicit lifecycle gaps. Bounded indexed later relations and agency/court leads are qualified search context, not an exhaustive internet or legal proof. Independent different-family review and owner-merged adoption remain separate gates.","recommended_provisional_status":"no","reconciliation_tasks":[{"canonical_binding":"missing_or_unreadable","detail":"Reconcile evidence, history and frozen base-dossier identities and seals; no status or review approval inferred.","expected_sha256":null,"path":"research/excavator/machiavelli-modern-undecided-20261105/b02/packet-v1/coordinator-v2/EO_13836/EVIDENCE.json","state":"missing_or_unreadable_evidence"}],"remaining_questions":["Whole-lifecycle inventory, first endings, continuing predicates, savings and counterevidence still require scoped research and independent review.","Reconcile stale or mismatched review: research/excavator/machiavelli-modern-undecided-20261105/b02/packet-v1/overlay/v1/EO_13836/STATUS_REVIEW.json","Reconcile stale or mismatched review: research/excavator/machiavelli-modern-undecided-20261105/b02/packet-v1/status-review-v1/EO_13836/STATUS_REVIEW.json","Reconcile producer evidence/history binding: research/excavator/machiavelli-modern-undecided-20261105/b02/packet-v1/coordinator-v2/EO_13836/EVIDENCE.json"],"reviews":[{"basis_sources":[],"basis_spans_pointer":null,"checked_evidence_ids":["ORIGINAL:EO:13836","ACT:EO:13836>EO:14003"],"d6":{},"dependency_state":"reconciliation_required","file_sha256":"e612d540341f695c28b19e9760a62b098d2dd23e077c124541a20a1bfb382d0c","full_lifecycle_approved":false,"path":"research/excavator/machiavelli-modern-undecided-20261105/b02/packet-v1/overlay/v1/EO_13836/STATUS_REVIEW.json","pointer":"","propositions":[],"rationale":"The exact decision's provisional NO recommendation is supported within the supplied source perimeter. EO 13836 §§1–9, 83 FR 25329–25334 (PDF pp.1–6). Section 3 establishes the labor-relations group, member assistance, information/model rules/analysis and an 18-month report. Sections 4–5 impose bargaining objectives, reports, procedures and agency-head review; §6 prohibits permissive-subject negotiations; §7 limits procedures/arrangements bargaining; §8 requires CBA/award submissions and public access. Timetables and completed agreements cannot exhaust this continuing package by themselves. EO 14003 §3(a), 86 FR 7231 (PDF p.1), revokes EO 13836, disbands its working group, and directs withdrawal of inconsistent group materials. Sections 3(e)–(f), PDF pp.1–2, address agency implementation separately. This covers all original commands rather than only disbanding the group. EO 13836 §9(c) preserves CBAs already in effect, and §9(a) preserves statutory authority. A surviving agreement does not maintain an independent EO-command regime. EO 14003's qualified cleanup allows agency/legal implementation issues to persist without qualifying its express revocation. EO 14148 §2(o), PDF p.2, revokes EO 14003, while §3(b), p.5, directs review and possible further action. Full text supplies no affirmative revival of EO 13836 or its group. Similar future policy or the continued existence of a CBA would require separate evidence.","reviewer":"astra-machiavelli-b02-independent-status-review","scope":"Independent research review only; never an eo-lifecycle-review/v1 certification.","verdict":"accept","version_binding":"stale_or_mismatched"},{"basis_sources":[],"basis_spans_pointer":null,"checked_evidence_ids":["ORIGINAL:EO:13836","ACT:EO:13836>EO:14003"],"d6":{},"dependency_state":"reconciliation_required","file_sha256":"96a740c9d4b654650aada36ecab2cb045b14325f20be8315d667f828e13c79fa","full_lifecycle_approved":false,"path":"research/excavator/machiavelli-modern-undecided-20261105/b02/packet-v1/status-review-v1/EO_13836/STATUS_REVIEW.json","pointer":"","propositions":[],"rationale":"The exact decision's provisional NO recommendation is supported within the supplied source perimeter. Preparation attestations never approve legal conclusions or replace whole-case review.
